<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-opus-4-7" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Opus 4.7</span><span class='zh'>Claude Opus 4.7 版本发布</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Opus 4.7 advances developer-focused AI capabilities with stronger reasoning, safety features, and tooling for complex agent workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Opus 4.7 在推理、安全性和代理工作流工具方面提升，面向开发者的 AI 助手更强大。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Opus 4.7 advances developer-focused AI capabilities with stronger reasoning, safety features, and tooling for complex agent workflows. It signals Anthropic&#x27;s push toward more capable, enterprise-ready assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Opus 4.7 在推理、安全性和代理工作流工具方面提升，面向开发者的 AI 助手更强大。此次更新体现了 Anthropic 向企业级应用扩展能力的方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/google-broke-its-promise-me-now-ice-has-my-data" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google broke its promise to me – now ICE has my data</span><span class='zh'>谷歌违背承诺，现在 ICE 已掌握我的数据</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy. The case underscores the tension between platform commitments and law enforcement data demands, and the need for stronger privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。此事凸显平台承诺与执法需求之间的矛盾，强调需要更强的隐私控制与透明度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-design-anthropic-labs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Design</span><span class='zh'>Claude 设计</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams. It brings Claude&#x27;s reasoning to brainstorming, prototyping, and visual generation with collaboration-friendly features and safety safeguards. The release signals AI tools maturing into specialist roles within design and product development.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。它将 Claude 的推理能力用于头脑风暴、原型设计和视觉生成，并具备协作功能与安全保护。该发布表明 AI 工具正在向设计和产品开发的专业化角色演进。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone bought 30 WordPress plugins and planted a backdoor in all of them</span><span class='zh'>有人买下 30 个 WordPress 插件并在全部插件中植入后门</span></a><span class="dom">anchor.host</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one. The stunt highlights how third-party extensions can compromise many sites if integrity checks and monitoring are lax.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。这暴露了第三方插件的供应链和信任机制漏洞，强调加强插件审计、完整性校验和运行时监控的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blackmagic Design expands DaVinci Resolve with a photo-focused workflow, integrating still-image editing and color tooling into the suite.</span><span class='zh'>Blackmagic Design 将 DaVinci Resolve 引入面向照片的工作流，将静态图像编辑和色彩分级工具整合到套件中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738883" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Docker pull fails in Spain due to football Cloudflare block</span><span class='zh'>西班牙因 Cloudflare 阻断导致 Docker pull 失败</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows. It underscores the need for resilient mirrors and alternative routing.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。需要更稳健的镜像源与路由策略以缓解。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Backblaze stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders, and possibly other cloud storage.</span><span class='zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive 和 Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Backblaze stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders, and possibly other cloud storage. This leaves cloud-native data unprotected by Backblaze and shifts the onus onto users to verify coverage and seek alternative backups.</span><span class='zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive 和 Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储。此举让这些云端数据不再受 Backblaze 的保护，用户需核对覆盖范围并考虑替代备选方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/codex-for-almost-everything/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex for almost everything</span><span class='zh'>几乎适用于一切场景的 Codex</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators. The move signals growing demand for single-model copilots that can handle coding, data tasks, automation, and workflow integration, reducing context-switching.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。此举反映出市场对单一模型兼顾编码、数据处理、自动化与工作流整合的需求日增，减少上下文切换。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam. The policy provides examples and enforcement expectations, encouraging developers to avoid overlays and deceptive navigation.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。政策给出示例和执行预期，鼓励开发者避免覆盖层、强制导航等误导性设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-stack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Stacked PRs</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 堆叠 PR</span></a><span class="dom">github.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally. The gh-stack tool provides commands to create, rebase, and track the stack, helping reviewers stay in sync across related changes.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。gh-stack 提供创建、变基和跟踪等命令，使评审人能够在相关改动之间保持同步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</span><span class='zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported misconfiguration left customer project files publicly accessible and searchable, exposing potentially sensitive data and trade secrets.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称 Fiverr 的配置错误使客户文件公开且可被检索，暴露潜在的敏感数据与商业机密。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported misconfiguration left customer project files publicly accessible and searchable, exposing potentially sensitive data and trade secrets. The incident underscores the risk of data leakage in freelancing marketplaces and the importance of rapid remediation and audit.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称 Fiverr 的配置错误使客户文件公开且可被检索，暴露潜在的敏感数据与商业机密。事件凸显自由职业市场的数据泄露风险，以及需要快速修复与审计的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.google.com/intl/en/ipv6/statistics.html?yzh=28197" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IPv6 traffic crosses the 50% mark</span><span class='zh'>IPv6 流量突破 50%</span></a><span class="dom">google.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers.</span><span class='zh'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers. The milestone reduces pressure on IPv4, but many operators still wrestle with dual-stack management and security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。这个里程碑减少了对 IPv4 的依赖，但运营商在双栈管理与安全性方面仍面临挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ban the sale of precise geolocation</span><span class='zh'>是时候禁止精确地理位置数据的买卖</span></a><span class="dom">lawfaremedia.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer. It calls for tighter data-broker accountability, with attention to enforcement and alternatives for legitimate use cases like emergency services.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。讨论执法难点与对紧急服务等正当用途的平衡，强调需要更严格的监管框架。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</span><span class='zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Isaac Asimov: The Last Question (1956)</span><span class='zh'>艾萨克·阿西莫夫：《最后的问题》（1956）</span></a><span class="dom">hex.ooo</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs. The narrative foreshadows core AI issues—scaling capabilities, interpretability, and the cost of pursuing unbounded optimization. It remains a touchstone for long-term thinking about technology&#x27;s reach and risks.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。故事预示了 AI 扩展能力、可解释性以及追求无界优化的代价等核心议题。至今，它仍是关于技术影响及长期风险思考的重要参照。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>732</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>288</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47792718" data-id="47792718" data-tags="Politics|Programming" data-score="720" data-comments="753" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718" data-time="1776346333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f97316' data-tag='Politics'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=aphyr.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>aphyr.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Where do we go from here?</span><span class='zh'>未来一切都是谎言？我们该往何处走？</span></a><span class="dom">aphyr.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology. He advocates clearer accountability, honest risk assessment, and building robust systems rather than chasing sensational predictions.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。倡导明确的问责、真实的风险评估，以及构建鲁棒系统，而非追捧耸人听闻的预言。</span></p>

These routines aim to accelerate common programming tasks and demonstrate practical patterns for applying language models in real-world coding workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 发布了一系列代码例程——模块化模板与函数，用于构建 AI 助力的软件。这些例程旨在加速常见编程任务，展示将大语言模型应用于实际编码工作流的实用模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/13/thousands-of-rare-concert-recordings-are-landing-on-the-internet-archive-listen-now/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive</span><span class='zh'>罕见演唱会录音正登陆互联网档案馆</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thousands of rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive, expanding its treasure trove of live performances.</span><span class='zh'>数千份罕见的演唱会录音正加入互联网档案馆，丰富了其现场演出藏品。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thousands of rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive, expanding its treasure trove of live performances. The influx broadens access for fans and researchers, but raises questions about rights, licensing, and long-term preservation.</span><span class='zh'>数千份罕见的演唱会录音正加入互联网档案馆，丰富了其现场演出藏品。增加的内容让粉丝和研究者获取更加广泛，但也引发关于版权、许可与长期保存的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/en/menu/burger/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The buns in McDonald&#x27;s Japan&#x27;s burger photos are all slightly askew</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞漢堡照片中的麵包略微歪斜</span></a><span class="dom">mcdonalds.co.jp</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew. The misalignment may be a photography quirk or a production reality, but it sparks discussion about brand presentation versus the actual product. In an era of highly scrutinized visuals, tiny imperfections can become talking points about authenticity in marketing.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。這種偏差可能是拍攝風格或製作過程造成，但卻引發對品牌呈現與實物之間差異的討論。在社群媒體高度放大的情境中，這種細微瑕疵也會成為關於真實感的話題。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opt-out request to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact highlights ongoing concerns about how browsers collect data and potential domestic data-sharing arrangements. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Seven countries now generate nearly all their electricity from renewables (2024)</span><span class='zh'>七个国家2024年几乎用可再生能源发电全部电力</span></a><span class="dom">the-independent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks. While intermittency remains a challenge, these cases show that with grid investments and capacity planning, a transition to low-carbon electricity is increasingly feasible at national scales.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。尽管间歇性仍是挑战，但这些案例表明通过电网投资和容量规划，国家层面的低碳发电转型正变得更加可行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</span><span class='zh'>回归地道设计</span></a><span class="dom">essays.johnloeber.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The local LLM ecosystem doesn’t need Ollama</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型生态不需要 Ollama</span></a><span class="dom">sleepingrobots.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance. It weighs trade-offs in updates, platform compatibility, and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。文章比较了更新、平台兼容性和生态成熟度等权衡，指出 Ollama 可能并非必需。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vercel disclosed a security incident from April 2026, detailing what happened, the affected services, and the steps they took to contain and remediate. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</span></a><span class="dom">archive.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>jj is a new command-line interface for Jujutsu, aiming to simplify interaction with the Jujutsu framework.</span><span class='zh'>jj 是一个新的 Jujutsu 命令行界面，旨在简化与 Jujutsu 框架的交互。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>jj is a new command-line interface for Jujutsu, aiming to simplify interaction with the Jujutsu framework. It provides a convenient, scriptable way to perform core tasks and experiments, reflecting a broader trend toward CLI-first tooling in niche systems.</span><span class='zh'>jj 是一个新的 Jujutsu 命令行界面，旨在简化与 Jujutsu 框架的交互。它提供一个便捷、可脚本化的方式来执行核心任务和实验，体现了在小众系统中向 CLI 优先工具的总体趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity looks like proof of work now</span><span class='zh'>網路安全如今看起來像工作量證明</span></a><span class="dom">dbreunig.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics. Defenders must continually invest to detect, deter, and recover from breaches, creating an ongoing arms race with threat actors. This perspective helps explain why security budgets tend to expand with rising threat capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。防禦方需要不斷投入資源來偵測、阻擊與修復破口，與威脅方的能力共同推動成本上升。以此觀點看待企業安全預算的成長具有說服力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://boringbar.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: boringBar – a taskbar-style dock replacement for macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：boringBar——macOS 的任务栏风格 Dock 替代</span></a><span class="dom">boringbar.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface.</span><span class='zh'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface. It emphasizes persistent pinned apps, quick-launch, and consistent shortcuts across spaces, offering an alternative UX to Apple’s native Dock. If it gains traction, it signals demand for more modular, multitasking-oriented macOS UI.</span><span class='zh'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。它为多任务工作提供了新的交互方式，若受欢迎，可能反映出对更模块化、以多任务为导向的 macOS UI 的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/jTGbKKGqs5EdyYoRc/most-people-can-t-juggle-one-ball" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Most people can&#x27;t juggle one ball</span><span class='zh'>大多数人都难以同时完成一个任务</span></a><span class="dom">lesswrong.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues cognitive limits cap how many things we can track or manage; multitasking often reduces performance even with simple tasks.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张人类在同时处理多项任务时存在认知极限，哪怕是处理一个简单任务也会有困难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues cognitive limits cap how many things we can track or manage; multitasking often reduces performance even with simple tasks. This finding has implications for UX and product design, reminding designers not to promise effortless multitasking.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张人类在同时处理多项任务时存在认知极限，哪怕是处理一个简单任务也会有困难。这一发现对用户体验和产品设计有启示：避免把多任务承诺得过于美好，需更真实地考虑人类注意力与工作记忆的边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://darkbloom.dev" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Darkbloom – Private inference on idle Macs</span><span class='zh'>Darkbloom：在闲置 Mac 上进行私有推断</span></a><span class="dom">darkbloom.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Darkbloom enables private ML inference to run on idle Macs, turning unused compute into on-device AI.</span><span class='zh'>Darkbloom 让闲置的 Mac 上也能运行私有化的机器学习推断，将空闲计算力转化为本地 AI 处理，避免将数据送往云端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Darkbloom enables private ML inference to run on idle Macs, turning unused compute into on-device AI. It emphasizes local data processing and privacy by avoiding cloud inference, illustrating a path for consumer hardware to support private AI workloads. If broadly adopted, it could shift how individuals contribute to AI without exposing data.</span><span class='zh'>Darkbloom 让闲置的 Mac 上也能运行私有化的机器学习推断，将空闲计算力转化为本地 AI 处理，避免将数据送往云端。此类方案强调本地数据处理与隐私，展示了利用日常硬件承载私有 AI 的可能路径。若普及，或改变个人对 AI 的参与方式，降低外部数据暴露风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sterlingcrispin/nothing-ever-happens" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nothing Ever Happens: Polymarket bot that always buys No on non-sports markets</span><span class='zh'>没有什么事会发生：一个在非体育市场总是买‘否’的 Polymarket 机器人</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Polymarket bot consistently places &#x27;No&#x27; bets on non-sports markets, revealing a recurring pattern in market dynamics that automated strategies can exploit.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Polymarket 机器人在非体育市场持续以‘否’下注，暴露了市场机制中的漏洞和自动化策略的潜在影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Polymarket bot consistently places &#x27;No&#x27; bets on non-sports markets, revealing a recurring pattern in market dynamics that automated strategies can exploit. This raises questions about prediction-market design and how such bots can skew odds or drain liquidity.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Polymarket 机器人在非体育市场持续以‘否’下注，暴露了市场机制中的漏洞和自动化策略的潜在影响。此现象引发对预测市场设计、赔率偏差以及流动性消耗的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.esa.int/Science_Exploration/Human_and_Robotic_Exploration/The_toxic_side_of_the_Moon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All 12 moonwalkers had &quot;lunar hay fever&quot; from dust smelling like gunpowder (2018)</span><span class='zh'>所有12名登月宇航员因月尘散发的火药味而出现类似花粉热的症状</span></a><span class="dom">esa.int</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ESA notes that all 12 Apollo astronauts reported hay-fever-like symptoms linked to lunar dust, which carries a gunpowder-like odor.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲航天局报告称，所有12名阿波罗任务宇航员都出现了与月尘相关的打喷嚏样症状，月尘散发着类似火药的气味。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ESA notes that all 12 Apollo astronauts reported hay-fever-like symptoms linked to lunar dust, which carries a gunpowder-like odor. The dust is chemically reactive and potentially toxic, posing health risks for future Moon missions and influencing habitat and suit design. Understanding its toxicity informs protective strategies for crews and equipment.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲航天局报告称，所有12名阿波罗任务宇航员都出现了与月尘相关的打喷嚏样症状，月尘散发着类似火药的气味。月尘具有化学反应性和潜在毒性，对未来月球任务的健康与安全构成风险，并影响基地、宇航服的设计。了解其毒性有助于制定对乘员和设备的防护策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kotaku.com/video-game-devs-explain-how-pausing-works-and-sometimes-it-gets-weird-2000686339" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Game devs explain the tricks involved with letting you pause a game</span><span class='zh'>游戏开发者揭秘暂停游戏背后的技术技巧</span></a><span class="dom">kotaku.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pausing a game is more than freezing the screen.</span><span class='zh'>暂停游戏不仅仅是将画面停下。需要精确地暂停游戏循环、物理仿真和计时器，同时保持音频等子系统的连贯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pausing a game is more than freezing the screen. It requires carefully suspending the game loop, physics, and timers while keeping audio and other subsystems coherent. In multiplayer and lockstep scenarios, pausing becomes far from trivial, so developers use tricks like time scaling, separate render-only states, or input buffering to ensure a consistent experience.</span><span class='zh'>暂停游戏不仅仅是将画面停下。需要精确地暂停游戏循环、物理仿真和计时器，同时保持音频等子系统的连贯。在多人或锁步场景下，暂停远非易事，开发者会使用时间缩放、仅渲染状态分离或输入缓冲等技巧来保证体验的一致性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://adlrocha.substack.com/p/adlrocha-how-the-ai-loser-may-end" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s accidental moat: How the &quot;AI Loser&quot; may end up winning</span><span class='zh'>苹果的意外护城河：AI 落后者也许会赢</span></a><span class="dom">adlrocha.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s cautious stance on AI could become a durable moat, built on privacy, on-device AI, and a tightly integrated ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>苹果对 AI 热潮的谨慎态度可能成为长期护城河，源自隐私保护、设备端 AI 与紧密的生态系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s cautious stance on AI could become a durable moat, built on privacy, on-device AI, and a tightly integrated ecosystem. As competitors chase scale, Apple&#x27;s trust advantage and user experience may prove sturdier over the long term.</span><span class='zh'>苹果对 AI 热潮的谨慎态度可能成为长期护城河，源自隐私保护、设备端 AI 与紧密的生态系统。随着竞争对手追逐规模，苹果在信任与用户体验方面的优势或在长期内更为稳固。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/04/any-color-you-nist-scientists-create-any-wavelength-lasers-tiny-circuits" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NIST scientists create &#x27;any wavelength&#x27; lasers</span><span class='zh'>任意颜色任你选：NIST科学家研制“任意波长”激光器</span></a><span class="dom">nist.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NIST researchers demonstrate on-chip tunable lasers capable of emitting a wide range of wavelengths from a single device, enabling compact, multi-wavelength light sources.</span><span class='zh'>NIST 展示了能在芯片上实现多波长输出的可调激光器，单一器件即可覆盖多条波长，适合光谱、传感和通信等领域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NIST researchers demonstrate on-chip tunable lasers capable of emitting a wide range of wavelengths from a single device, enabling compact, multi-wavelength light sources. This could reduce system size and cost for spectroscopy, sensing, and communications, accelerating advances in integrated photonics.</span><span class='zh'>NIST 展示了能在芯片上实现多波长输出的可调激光器，单一器件即可覆盖多条波长，适合光谱、传感和通信等领域。此类微型电路有望降低系统体积与成本，推动集成光子技术的发展。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/Monthly%20cost%20vs%20value.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='viktorcessan.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">67</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The economics of software teams: Why most engineering orgs are flying blind</span><span class='zh'>软件团队的经济学：为何多数工程组织在盲目运作</span></a><span class="dom">viktorcessan.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Most engineering organizations lack visibility into the true cost of software delivery, often optimizing for velocity rather than value.</span><span class='zh'>工程组织往往对软件交付的真实成本缺乏清晰度，常以追求速度为目标而忽视价值。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Most engineering organizations lack visibility into the true cost of software delivery, often optimizing for velocity rather than value. The piece argues that without product-aligned incentives and cost-aware metrics, teams accumulate technical debt and misallocate resources. It offers practical levers like measuring feature economics, improving cross-functional collaboration, and building dashboards that surface cycle time, cost per feature, and delivered impact.</span><span class='zh'>工程组织往往对软件交付的真实成本缺乏清晰度，常以追求速度为目标而忽视价值。若缺乏以产品为导向的激励与成本感知的指标，团队容易积累技术债务、资源错配。文中提出通过衡量功能经济学、提高跨职能协作，以及建立能反映交付成本、周期时间和实际影响的看板等来纠偏。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/04/android-now-stops-you-sharing-your-location-in-photos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android now stops you sharing your location in photos</span><span class='zh'>Android 现在阻止你在照片中分享位置信息</span></a><span class="dom">shkspr.mobi</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Android now strips location data from photos before sharing, helping protect user privacy when posting to social apps.</span><span class='zh'>Android 新功能在分享照片前移除位置信息元数据，帮助用户避免地理位置信息泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Android now strips location data from photos before sharing, helping protect user privacy when posting to social apps. The change reduces inadvertent location leaks and sets a baseline for privacy controls across mobile platforms, though users can still re-enable when needed.</span><span class='zh'>Android 新功能在分享照片前移除位置信息元数据，帮助用户避免地理位置信息泄露。该改动提升隐私保护，但仍允许需要时开启位置标记的选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/unexpected-54k-billing-spike-in-13-hours-firebase-browser-key-without-api-restrictions-used-for-gemini-requests/140262" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>€54k spike in 13h from unrestricted Firebase browser key accessing Gemini APIs</span><span class='zh'>13小时内因未受限的 Firebase 浏览器密钥访问 Gemini API 导致€54k 的账单激增</span></a><span class="dom">discuss.ai.google.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An unrestricted Firebase browser key caused a €54k Gemini API bill in about 13 hours, illustrating the cost risk of lax credentials.</span><span class='zh'>未受限的 Firebase 浏览器密钥在约13小时内触发对 Gemini API 的€54,000 账单，暴露了凭证管理的成本风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An unrestricted Firebase browser key caused a €54k Gemini API bill in about 13 hours, illustrating the cost risk of lax credentials. It underscores the need for strict key restrictions, monitoring, and secure config in cloud-integrated AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>未受限的 Firebase 浏览器密钥在约13小时内触发对 Gemini API 的€54,000 账单，暴露了凭证管理的成本风险。强调在云端集成 AI 工作负载中对密钥限制、监控和安全配置的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.righto.com/2026/04/B-52-star-tracker-angle-computer.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The electromechanical angle computer inside the B-52 bomber&#x27;s star tracker</span><span class='zh'>B-52 轰炸机星跟踪器内的机电角度计算机</span></a><span class="dom">righto.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A detailed look at the electromechanical angle computer housed inside the B-52&#x27;s star tracker.</span><span class='zh'>通过解读B-52轰炸机星跟踪器内的机电角度计算机，这篇文章展示了在没有数字处理器时，如何靠齿轮、继电器等部件实现航向姿态计算。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A detailed look at the electromechanical angle computer housed inside the B-52&#x27;s star tracker. It converts star-field data into aircraft attitude angles with gears and relays, a relic of pre-digital avionics ingenuity. The design shows how engineers built reliable navigation hardware under wartime constraints and hints at the evolution of embedded computing.</span><span class='zh'>通过解读B-52轰炸机星跟踪器内的机电角度计算机，这篇文章展示了在没有数字处理器时，如何靠齿轮、继电器等部件实现航向姿态计算。它揭示了早期航电系统在可靠性、响应时效与维护方面的权衡，以及嵌入式计算发展的一个有趣里程碑。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple Maps has reportedly removed most towns and villages from Lebanon, creating large gaps in local navigation data.</span><span class='zh'>报道称苹果地图在黎巴嫩几乎不再显示大多数城镇和村庄，导致本地导航数据出现明显空白。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple Maps has reportedly removed most towns and villages from Lebanon, creating large gaps in local navigation data. The move raises questions about data sourcing, geopolitical boundaries, and the reliability of mapping services in conflict zones, with potential knock-on effects for travel, logistics, and crisis response.</span><span class='zh'>报道称苹果地图在黎巴嫩几乎不再显示大多数城镇和村庄，导致本地导航数据出现明显空白。此举引发关于数据来源、界限划分以及冲突地区地图服务可靠性的关注，可能影响出行、物流和应急响应。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open source isn&#x27;t dead, but Cal.com demonstrates a flawed response to AI disruption.</span><span class='zh'>开源并未死去，但 Cal.com 的做法暴露了对 AI 变革的错误应对。</span></p>

The incident highlights variability in reputation metrics and the importance of robust email authentication practices.</span><span class='zh'>尽管某服务给出99%的邮件信誉分，但 Gmail 的判定并不认同，导致投递问题与误解。此事凸显邮件信誉评估之间的差异以及强化 DKIM/SPF 等认证、送达策略的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Notion reportedly leaked email addresses for editors of any public page, widening phishing and social-engineering risks for those editors.</span><span class='zh'>Notion 被曝公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址，扩大了他们遭遇钓鱼与社工攻击的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Notion reportedly leaked email addresses for editors of any public page, widening phishing and social-engineering risks for those editors. The exposure underscores how collaboration platforms can inadvertently leak contact data, prompting urgent checks on access, password hygiene, and alerting for targeted scams.</span><span class='zh'>Notion 被曝公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址，扩大了他们遭遇钓鱼与社工攻击的风险。此类暴露凸显协作工具对外共享联系信息的潜在隐患，需紧急检查权限、提升密码安全并警惕定向诈骗。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/16/everything-we-like-is-a-psyop/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Everything we like is a psyop?</span><span class='zh'>我们喜欢的一切都是心理战吗</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A TechCrunch piece argues that much of what we enjoy is shaped by psychological operations, highlighting how media, marketing, and social design influence taste and behavior.</span><span class='zh'>TechCrunch 指出，我们喜爱的很多东西可能被心理战操控，媒体、营销和社交设计对口味与行为具有放大效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A TechCrunch piece argues that much of what we enjoy is shaped by psychological operations, highlighting how media, marketing, and social design influence taste and behavior. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/18/iran-war-bets-ethics-concerns" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Traders placed over $1B in perfectly timed bets on the Iran war</span><span class='zh'>交易者在伊朗战争上下注超过10亿美元，时机极其精准</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece reports that traders placed more than a billion dollars in perfectly timed bets tied to the Iran war.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出交易者在伊朗战争相关的事件上下了超过10亿美元的注码，时机极为精准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece reports that traders placed more than a billion dollars in perfectly timed bets tied to the Iran war. It underscores the tension between speed and scrutiny in crisis-driven trading.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出交易者在伊朗战争相关的事件上下了超过10亿美元的注码，时机极为精准。此事揭示地缘政治事件如何带来高额套利机会，也引发对市场伦理和透明度的质疑。强调在危机驱动的交易中，速度与监管之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://victorpoughon.github.io/interval-calculator/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I made a calculator that works over disjoint sets of intervals</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个在不相交区间集合上运算的计算器</span></a><span class="dom">victorpoughon.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Inputs are disjoint intervals; arithmetic is performed piecewise, returning a union of intervals.</span><span class='zh'>把输入视作不相交区间的并集，逐段进行运算，结果仍然是区间的并集。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Inputs are disjoint intervals; arithmetic is performed piecewise, returning a union of intervals. This demonstrates practical interval arithmetic and could enable robust numeric reasoning in uncertain data, scheduling windows, or streaming computations.</span><span class='zh'>把输入视作不相交区间的并集，逐段进行运算，结果仍然是区间的并集。这样的区间算术在处理不确定数据、时间窗口调度或流式计算时具有实际应用价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paniclock/paniclock/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PanicLock – Close your MacBook lid disable TouchID –&gt; password unlock</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock：合上 MacBook 盖时禁用 Touch ID，改用密码解锁</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PanicLock is a small utility that disables Touch ID when the MacBook lid is closed and requires a password to unlock.</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock 是一个小型工具，合上 MacBook 盖时会禁用指纹解锁，解锁需输入密码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PanicLock is a small utility that disables Touch ID when the MacBook lid is closed and requires a password to unlock. It provides a simple privacy safeguard for scenarios like lending or transporting devices, though it adds an extra unlock step.</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock 是一个小型工具，合上 MacBook 盖时会禁用指纹解锁，解锁需输入密码。这为借出或携带设备等场景提供了简单的隐私保护，但也增加了额外的解锁步骤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/citguru/openduck" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Distributed DuckDB Instance</span><span class='zh'>分布式 DuckDB 实例</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A distributed DuckDB instance aims to run analytical queries across multiple nodes, enabling larger-scale analytics than a single process.</span><span class='zh'>提出在多节点上运行 DuckDB 的分布式实例，以并行化分析查询、突破单进程的容量限制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A distributed DuckDB instance aims to run analytical queries across multiple nodes, enabling larger-scale analytics than a single process. This direction tackles data distribution, parallel execution, and fault tolerance, signaling scalable analytics for analytics workloads.</span><span class='zh'>提出在多节点上运行 DuckDB 的分布式实例，以并行化分析查询、突破单进程的容量限制。该方向需解决数据分布、并行执行和故障恢复等挑战，代表分析型工作负载的可扩展性探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NikolayS/pgque" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PgQue: Zero-Bloat Postgres Queue</span><span class='zh'>PgQue：零臃肿的 PostgreSQL 队列</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introducing PgQue, a lightweight PostgreSQL-backed queue with a tiny footprint designed for reliable background processing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 PgQue，一种极简且零膨胀的 PostgreSQL 队列实现，专注于可靠的后台处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introducing PgQue, a lightweight PostgreSQL-backed queue with a tiny footprint designed for reliable background processing. Emphasizes simplicity, ease of use, and predictable performance for apps needing queueing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 PgQue，一种极简且零膨胀的 PostgreSQL 队列实现，专注于可靠的后台处理。强调简洁性、易用性和可预测的性能，适合需要排队能力的应用场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MDV adds data-aware blocks to Markdown to support docs, dashboards, and data-driven slides, making content both readable and runnable. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The SDL project bans commits authored by AI, citing concerns about provenance and reproducibility, pushing contributors to ensure human-generated edits.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 项目禁止 AI 自动生成的提交，基于可追溯性与可重复性等顾虑，要求贡献者确保人为编辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The SDL project bans commits authored by AI, citing concerns about provenance and reproducibility, pushing contributors to ensure human-generated edits.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 项目禁止 AI 自动生成的提交，基于可追溯性与可重复性等顾虑，要求贡献者确保人为编辑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dropseed/plain" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Plain – The full-stack Python framework designed for humans and agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Plain——为人和代理人设计的全栈 Python 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Plain is a full-stack Python framework designed for humans and AI agents building apps.</span><span class='zh'>Plain 是一个为人和 AI 代理人打造应用的全栈 Python 框架，强调开发者体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Plain is a full-stack Python framework designed for humans and AI agents building apps. It emphasizes developer ergonomics, offering end-to-end tooling, clear abstractions, and smooth integration with AI workflows to speed up common tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Plain 是一个为人和 AI 代理人打造应用的全栈 Python 框架，强调开发者体验。它提供端到端工具、清晰的抽象以及与 AI 工作流的无缝整合，帮助加速常见任务的实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mwallner/mergetopus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Git helper tool that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks</span><span class='zh'>将大合并拆分为可并行任务的 Git 助手工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mergetopus is a Git helper that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks, enabling faster integration and more scalable conflict resolution on big repositories.</span><span class='zh'>Mergetopus 将大型 Git 合并拆分为可并行的任务，从而在大型代码库中实现更快的合并、以及更具扩展性的冲突解决。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mergetopus is a Git helper that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks, enabling faster integration and more scalable conflict resolution on big repositories. It distributes work across cores or workers to boost throughput during merges.</span><span class='zh'>Mergetopus 将大型 Git 合并拆分为可并行的任务，从而在大型代码库中实现更快的合并、以及更具扩展性的冲突解决。它将工作分发到多核或多线程以提升吞吐量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nyigoro/lumina-lang" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lumina – a statically typed web-native language for JavaScript and WASM</span><span class='zh'>Lumina：面向 JavaScript 与 WASM 的静态类型化网页原生语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lumina is a statically typed web-native language designed for web targets, interoperating with JavaScript and WebAssembly.</span><span class='zh'>Lumina 是一门面向网页原生的静态类型语言，设计用于与 JavaScript 和 WebAssembly 的互操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lumina is a statically typed web-native language designed for web targets, interoperating with JavaScript and WebAssembly. The language aims to improve safety and performance for web apps while keeping developer ergonomics.</span><span class='zh'>Lumina 是一门面向网页原生的静态类型语言，设计用于与 JavaScript 和 WebAssembly 的互操作。它旨在在提升网络应用安全性与性能的同时，保持开发者的使用体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yapstudios/sfsym" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sfsym – Export Apple SF Symbols as Vector SVG/PDF/PNG</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Sfsym 将 Apple SF Symbols 导出为矢量格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Sfsym, a tool that exports Apple SF Symbols to scalable vector formats (SVG, PDF) or PNG for design workflows.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Sfsym，这是一个将 Apple 的 SF Symbols 导出为可缩放矢量格式（SVG、PDF）或 PNG 的工具，方便设计工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Sfsym, a tool that exports Apple SF Symbols to scalable vector formats (SVG, PDF) or PNG for design workflows. Enables designers to integrate system icons into custom UI assets without manual tracing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Sfsym，这是一个将 Apple 的 SF Symbols 导出为可缩放矢量格式（SVG、PDF）或 PNG 的工具，方便设计工作流。让设计师能够在自定义 UI 资源中直接使用系统图标，而无需手动追踪。</span></p>
